 Research Plans for 1960·61.
   . As indicated in the accompanyin_g progress reports and papers
 related to the research supported by the grant, several crystalline
 substance have been isolated from Amanita Musca.r1a and Rivea. Corymbosa.
 P\lture vork vill be orge.nize·i +..o accomplish the following obJectives:
      1.   Ascertain, by means of biological testing, which, if any, of
           the crystalline materials exhibft significant physiological
           effects in test ·animals. ('Ibis
           ~ision of Dr                                      th._
                                                         ca.rri~ .· t!J./ (!__.
                                                        .t.

      2.   Complete fractionation and purification of ~rude concentrates
           which exhibit physiological effects in test animals •. When
           pure crystalline substances are isolated they will be identi·
           fied or structural elucidation will be attempted.
      3· For those substances, which exhibit significant physiological
           effects and vhicll have. novel structure 1 an attempt vill be
           made to synthesize them and compounds with related structure
           in order that relative physiological activity can be deter-
           mined~   ·

    lack of suffiCient quantlties of plant material the amount isolated bas

    been insufficient for complete structure elucidation. EC!ort durlng

    tration procedures.         Chemical and biological techniques will be                "
